var sh = new ActiveXObject('WScript.Shell');
var bat = WScript.Arguments(0);
var w = false;
var a = '';
for (var i = 1; i < WScript.Arguments.length; i++) if (WScript.Arguments(i) == '__wait__') w = true; else a += ' ' + WScript.Arguments(i);
sh.Run('cmd /c "' + bat + '"' + a, 0, w);
WScript.Quit(0);
